[POS4] === JUNO ENGINE B — S3 LISTEN FIRMWARE ===
[POS4] voices allowed: 6   sample rate: 44100
[POS4] free internal 120695  free PSRAM 8355844
[POS4] BUILD: DEVICE RECALL. The coefficients are built ON THIS CHIP from
[POS4]        134 compact patch bytes and a 30328 B baked cell array. There is
[POS4]        no frozen blob in this image.
[POS4] RECALL: boot recall done, patch 0, gen 1
[POS4] PLAY: boot chord released. The board is SILENT until you play a key. Patch stepping is OFF.
[POS4] E (874) task_wdt: delete_entry(240): task not found
[POS4] TWO CORES: voices 2..2 on core 0, 3..7 on core 1
[POS4] VCAPIPE: voice 3 audio half runs on core 0, one chunk late (chip output ships from the previous bank)
[POS4] KEYS: a..k play  z/x octave  b/n patch  ,/. split  r robot on/off  space all-notes-off  t fire the tooth
[POS4] 
[POS4] === CHAIN4: THIS BOARD IS POSITION 4 OF 4 (1 = the DAC end) ===
[POS4] CHAIN: renders GLOBAL voice slots 2..3 of 2..7; no master (NOMASTER); notes arrive over the event chain
[POS4] CHAIN: DOWN ctl TX 5 RX 6; DOWN audio slave TX TDM4 BCLK 15 LRCK 16 DATA 17
[POS4] CRCBENCH: byte=12685 slice4=18019 rom=10125 cyc/KB (rom==byte values: YES)
[POS4] CHAIN: every mix gate stays CLOSED until its hop is CRC-proven; wiring -> docs/engineb/CHAIN4.md §6
[POS4] TYPE TO PLAY -- no parts needed, this console IS the keyboard:
[POS4]           a s d f g h j k  = C D E F G A B C   (white keys)
[POS4]             w e   t y u    = the black keys
[POS4]           z / x = octave down / up     SPACE = release all
[POS4]           b / n = previous / next patch  (all 64 factory patches;
[POS4]                   a program change costs the full burst, so a
[POS4]                   click there is expected and is measured)
